198	/* ------------------------------- Unless drill ------------------------------ */
199	
200	export type UnlessPattern = 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
201	
202	export const UNLESS_LETTERS = ["A", "B", "C", "D", "P", "Q", "R", "S"];
203	
204	export interface UnlessRound {
205	  pattern: UnlessPattern;
206	  x: string;
207	  y: string;
208	}
209	
210	/** Statement text pieces; "/" marks the slashed letter. */
211	export function unlessStatement({ pattern, x, y }: UnlessRound): string {
212	  switch (pattern) {
213	    case 1: return `/${x} unless ${y}`;
214	    case 2: return `Unless ${y}, ${x}`;
215	    case 3: return `Unless /${y}, /${x}`;
216	    case 4: return `${x} unless /${y}`;
217	  }
218	}
219	
220	export function unlessAnswer({ pattern, x, y }: UnlessRound): { left: string; right: string } {
221	  switch (pattern) {
222	    case 1: return { left: x, right: y };
223	    case 2: return { left: `/${x}`, right: y };
224	    case 3: return { left: x, right: `/${y}` };
225	    case 4: return { left: `/${x}`, right: `/${y}` };
226	  }
227	}
228	
229	export function negateToken(token: string): string {
230	  return token.startsWith("/") ? token.slice(1) : `/${token}`;
231	}
232	
233	export function contrapositiveOf(left: string, right: string): { left: string; right: string } {
234	  return { left: negateToken(right), right: negateToken(left) };
235	}

1	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
2	import {
3	  DIAGRAM_ITEMS, FLASHCARDS, GROUPS, GROUP_LABEL, INDICATOR_POOL, UNLESS_LETTERS,
4	  contrapositiveOf, negateToken, unlessAnswer, unlessStatement,
5	  type DiagramItem, type GroupId, type UnlessPattern, type UnlessRound,
6	} from "./content";
7	import { Diagram, Side, Tok } from "./Notation";
8	import "./conditional-refresher.css";

10	const TABS = ["Cheat Sheet", "Sort Drills", "Flashcards", "Diagram Drills", "Unless Drill"] as const;
11	type Tab = (typeof TABS)[number];
12	
13	const KEY = "lsatu.conditional-refresher.v1";
14	
15	interface Saved {
16	  sort?: { done: number; correct: number };
17	  flash?: Record<string, "again" | "needs" | "got">;
18	  diagram?: { done: number; correct: number };
19	  unless?: { rounds: number };
20	}
21	
22	function readSaved(): Saved {
23	  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || "{}") as Saved; } catch { return {}; }
24	}
25	function writeSaved(patch: Saved) {
26	  try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify({ ...readSaved(), ...patch })); } catch { /* storage unavailable */ }
27	}
28	
29	function shuffle<T>(items: T[]): T[] {
30	  const a = [...items];
31	  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
32	    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
33	    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
34	  }
35	  return a;
36	}
37	
38	/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
39	
40	export default function ConditionalIndicatorsRefresher() {
41	  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("Cheat Sheet");
42	  return (
43	    <div className="cir-root">
44	      <div className="cir-shell">
45	        <h1 className="cir-title">Conditional indicators</h1>
46	        <p className="cir-sub">Your progress on this page is saved in this browser.</p>
47	        <div className="cir-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Conditional indicators sections">
48	          {TABS.map(t => (
49	            <button key={t} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={tab === t}
50	              className="cir-tab" onClick={() => setTab(t)}>{t}</button>
51	          ))}
52	        </div>
53	        {tab === "Cheat Sheet" && <CheatSheet />}
54	        {tab === "Sort Drills" && <SortDrills />}
55	        {tab === "Flashcards" && <Flashcards />}
56	        {tab === "Diagram Drills" && <DiagramDrills />}
57	        {tab === "Unless Drill" && <UnlessDrill />}
58	      </div>
59	    </div>
60	  );
61	}
62	
63	/* --------------------------------- Cheat ---------------------------------- */
64	
65	function CheatSheet() {
66	  return (
67	    <section>
68	      <p className="cir-lead">Review below</p>
69	      <div className="cir-quadrants">
70	        {GROUPS.map(g => (
71	          <article key={g.id} className="cir-quad">
72	            <div className="cir-quad-n">Group {g.n}</div>
73	            <h2>{g.title}</h2>
74	            <div className="cir-words">
75	              {g.words.map(w => <span key={w} className="cir-word">{w}</span>)}
76	            </div>
77	            {g.rule && <p>{g.rule}</p>}
78	            {g.steps && (
79	              <ol className="cir-steps">
80	                {g.steps.map(s => <li key={s}>{s}</li>)}
81	              </ol>
82	            )}
83	            {g.note && <p className="cir-note">{g.note}</p>}
84	          </article>
85	        ))}
86	      </div>
87	      <div className="cir-legend">
88	        <div>Negation: <Tok text="/A" /></div>
89	        <div>And: <Side text="A + B" /></div>
90	        <div>Or: <Side text="A or B" /></div>
91	        <div>Conditional: <Diagram left="A" right="/B" /></div>
92	      </div>
93	    </section>
94	  );
95	}
96	
97	/* ------------------------------- Sort drills ------------------------------- */
98	
99	function SortDrills() {
100	  const [queue, setQueue] = useState(() => shuffle(INDICATOR_POOL));
101	  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
102	  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<GroupId | null>(null);
103	  const [correct, setCorrect] = useState(0);
104	  const item = queue[index];
105	  const finished = index >= queue.length;
106	
107	  const choose = (group: GroupId) => {
108	    if (picked) return;
109	    setPicked(group);
110	    if (group === item.group) setCorrect(c => c + 1);
111	    writeSaved({ sort: { done: index + 1, correct: correct + (group === item.group ? 1 : 0) } });
112	  };
113	  const next = () => { setPicked(null); setIndex(i => i + 1); };
114	  const restart = () => { setQueue(shuffle(INDICATOR_POOL)); setIndex(0); setPicked(null); setCorrect(0); };
115	
116	  return (
117	    <section className="cir-card">
118	      <div className="cir-bar" role="progressbar" aria-label="Sort drill progress"
119	        aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={queue.length} aria-valuenow={Math.min(index, queue.length)}>
120	        <span style={{ width: `${Math.round((Math.min(index, queue.length) / queue.length) * 100)}%` }} />
121	      </div>
122	      {finished ? (
123	        <>
124	          <p className="cir-prompt">Pool complete.</p>
125	          <div className="cir-actions"><button type="button" className="cir-btn" onClick={restart}>Run it again</button></div>
126	        </>
127	      ) : (
128	        <>
129	          <p className="cir-meta">Which group?</p>
130	          <p className="cir-prompt">{item.word}</p>
131	          <div className="cir-choices">
132	            {GROUPS.map(g => {
133	              const state = !picked ? "" : g.id === item.group ? " is-right" : g.id === picked ? " is-wrong" : "";
134	              return (
135	                <button key={g.id} type="button" className={`cir-choice${state}`} disabled={!!picked}
136	                  onClick={() => choose(g.id)}>{GROUP_LABEL[g.id]}</button>
137	              );
138	            })}
139	          </div>
140	          {picked && (
141	            <>
142	              <p className="cir-feedback">
143	                {picked === item.group ? "Right." : "Not quite."} <strong>{item.word}</strong> is {GROUP_LABEL[item.group]}.
144	              </p>
145	              <div className="cir-actions"><button type="button" className="cir-btn" onClick={next}>Next</button></div>
146	            </>
147	          )}
148	        </>
149	      )}
150	    </section>
151	  );
152	}

156	function Flashcards() {
157	  const [queue, setQueue] = useState(() => shuffle(FLASHCARDS));
158	  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
159	  const [step, setStep] = useState<1 | 2 | 3>(1);
160	  const card = queue[index];
161	  const done = index >= queue.length;
162	
163	  const rate = (value: "again" | "needs" | "got") => {
164	    const flash = { ...(readSaved().flash ?? {}), [card.id]: value };
165	    writeSaved({ flash });
166	    if (value === "again") setQueue(q => [...q, card]);
167	    setStep(1);
168	    setIndex(i => i + 1);
169	  };
170	
171	  if (done) {
172	    return (
173	      <section className="cir-card">
174	        <p className="cir-prompt">Deck complete.</p>
175	        <div className="cir-actions">
176	          <button type="button" className="cir-btn" onClick={() => { setQueue(shuffle(FLASHCARDS)); setIndex(0); setStep(1); }}>Shuffle again</button>
177	        </div>
178	      </section>
179	    );
180	  }
181	
182	  return (
183	    <section className="cir-card">
184	      <div className="cir-bar"><span style={{ width: `${Math.round((index / queue.length) * 100)}%` }} /></div>
185	      <p className="cir-meta">Card {index + 1} of {queue.length}</p>
186	      <p className="cir-prompt">{card.front}</p>
187	      <div className="cir-step"><b>Step 1.</b> Translate it — write the diagram out on your paper before you go on.</div>
188	      {step >= 2 && (
189	        <div className="cir-step"><b>Step 2.</b> Say the diagram out loud as ordinary English: &ldquo;If &hellip;, then &hellip;&rdquo; using the actual complete ideas and every negative.</div>
190	      )}
191	      {step < 3 ? (
192	        <div className="cir-actions">
193	          <button type="button" className="cir-btn" onClick={() => setStep(s => (s === 1 ? 2 : 3))}>
194	            {step === 1 ? "I have the diagram" : "I said it in English"}
195	          </button>
196	        </div>
197	      ) : (
198	        <>
199	          <div className="cir-reveal">
200	            <div className="cir-reveal-row"><span>Conditional</span><p>{card.conditional}</p></div>
201	            <div className="cir-reveal-row"><span>Contrapositive</span><p>{card.contrapositive}</p></div>
202	            <div className="cir-reveal-row"><span>In English</span><p>{card.englishConditional}</p></div>
203	            <div className="cir-reveal-row"><span>Contrapositive in English</span><p>{card.englishContrapositive}</p></div>
204	            {card.note && <div className="cir-reveal-row"><span>Why</span><p>{card.note}</p></div>}
205	          </div>
206	          <div className="cir-actions">
207	            <button type="button" className="cir-btn ghost" onClick={() => rate("again")}>Again</button>
208	            <button type="button" className="cir-btn ghost" onClick={() => rate("needs")}>Needs work</button>
209	            <button type="button" className="cir-btn" onClick={() => rate("got")}>Got it</button>
210	          </div>
211	        </>
212	      )}
213	    </section>
214	  );
215	}
216	
217	/* ----------------------------- Diagram drills ------------------------------ */
218	
219	function sideContrapositive(text: string): string {
220	  if (text.includes(" + ")) return text.split(" + ").map(t => negateToken(t.trim())).join(" or ");
221	  if (text.includes(" or ")) return text.split(" or ").map(t => negateToken(t.trim())).join(" + ");
222	  return negateToken(text.trim());
223	}
224	
225	function itemContrapositive(item: DiagramItem): { left: string; right: string } {
226	  return { left: sideContrapositive(item.right), right: sideContrapositive(item.left) };
227	}
228	
229	const norm = (s: string) => s.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ").toLowerCase();
230	
231	function DiagramDrills() {
232	  const [queue, setQueue] = useState(() => shuffle(DIAGRAM_ITEMS));
233	  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
234	  const [side, setSide] = useState<"left" | "right">("left");
235	  const [left, setLeft] = useState<string[]>([]);
236	  const [right, setRight] = useState<string[]>([]);
237	  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<null | boolean>(null);
238	  const item = queue[index];
239	  const done = index >= queue.length;
240	
241	  const reset = useCallback(() => { setLeft([]); setRight([]); setSide("left"); setChecked(null); }, []);
242	  const push = (token: string) => {
243	    if (checked !== null) return;
244	    (side === "left" ? setLeft : setRight)(v => [...v, token]);
245	  };
246	  const undo = () => (side === "left" ? setLeft : setRight)(v => v.slice(0, -1));
247	
248	  const check = () => {
249	    const mine = { left: norm(left.join(" ")), right: norm(right.join(" ")) };
250	    const target = { left: norm(item.left), right: norm(item.right) };
251	    const contra = itemContrapositive(item);
252	    const ok = (mine.left === target.left && mine.right === target.right)
253	      || (mine.left === norm(contra.left) && mine.right === norm(contra.right));
254	    setChecked(ok);
255	    const saved = readSaved().diagram ?? { done: 0, correct: 0 };
256	    writeSaved({ diagram: { done: saved.done + 1, correct: saved.correct + (ok ? 1 : 0) } });
257	  };
258	
259	  if (done) {
260	    return (
261	      <section className="cir-card">
262	        <p className="cir-prompt">All diagram drills done.</p>
263	        <div className="cir-actions">
264	          <button type="button" className="cir-btn" onClick={() => { setQueue(shuffle(DIAGRAM_ITEMS)); setIndex(0); reset(); }}>Shuffle again</button>
265	        </div>
266	      </section>
267	    );
268	  }
269	
270	  const contra = itemContrapositive(item);
271	  return (
272	    <section className="cir-card">
273	      <div className="cir-bar"><span style={{ width: `${Math.round((index / queue.length) * 100)}%` }} /></div>
274	      <p className="cir-meta">Diagram it. Contrapositives count.</p>
275	      <p className="cir-prompt">{item.prompt}</p>
276	
277	      <div className="cir-build">
278	        <div className={`cir-slot${side === "left" ? " active" : ""}`} onClick={() => checked === null && setSide("left")}>
279	          {left.length ? left.map((t, i) => <Tok key={`${t}-${i}`} text={t} />) : <span className="cir-stack-join">left</span>}
280	        </div>
281	        <span className="cir-arrow">&rarr;</span>
282	        <div className={`cir-slot${side === "right" ? " active" : ""}`} onClick={() => checked === null && setSide("right")}>
283	          {right.length ? right.map((t, i) => <Tok key={`${t}-${i}`} text={t} />) : <span className="cir-stack-join">right</span>}
284	        </div>
285	      </div>
286	
287	      <div className="cir-side-toggle">
288	        <button type="button" className={`cir-btn ${side === "left" ? "" : "ghost"}`} onClick={() => setSide("left")}>Building left</button>
289	        <button type="button" className={`cir-btn ${side === "right" ? "" : "ghost"}`} onClick={() => setSide("right")}>Building right</button>
290	      </div>
291	
292	      <div className="cir-palette">
293	        {item.names.flatMap(name => [name, `/${name}`]).map(token => (
294	          <button key={token} type="button" className="cir-token" onClick={() => push(token)}><Tok text={token} /></button>
295	        ))}
296	        {(item.connectors ?? []).map(c => (
297	          <button key={c} type="button" className="cir-token" onClick={() => push(c)}>{c}</button>
298	        ))}
299	        <button type="button" className="cir-token" onClick={undo} disabled={checked !== null}>Undo</button>
300	      </div>
301	
302	      <div className="cir-actions">
303	        {checked === null ? (
304	          <button type="button" className="cir-btn" disabled={!left.length || !right.length} onClick={check}>Check</button>
305	        ) : (
306	          <button type="button" className="cir-btn" onClick={() => { setIndex(i => i + 1); reset(); }}>Next</button>
307	        )}
308	      </div>
309	
310	      {checked !== null && (
311	        <div className="cir-feedback">
312	          <p>{checked ? "Right." : "Not quite."}</p>
313	          <p>Conditional: <Diagram left={item.left} right={item.right} /></p>
314	          <p>Contrapositive: <Diagram left={contra.left} right={contra.right} /></p>
315	        </div>
316	      )}
317	    </section>
318	  );
319	}
320	
321	/* ------------------------------ Unless drill ------------------------------- */
322	
323	const WINDOW_MS = 5000;
324	
325	interface UnlessResult extends UnlessRound {
326	  statement: string;
327	  chosenLeft: string | null;
328	  chosenRight: string | null;
329	  seconds: number;
330	}
331	
332	function buildRound(): UnlessRound[] {
333	  const patterns = shuffle<UnlessPattern>([1, 2, 3, 4]).slice(0, 3);
334	  const letters = shuffle(UNLESS_LETTERS);
335	  return shuffle(patterns).map((pattern, i) => ({
336	    pattern,
337	    x: letters[(i * 2) % letters.length],
338	    y: letters[(i * 2 + 1) % letters.length],
339	  }));
340	}
341	
342	function UnlessDrill() {
343	  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<"idle" | "running" | "stop" | "summary">("idle");
344	  const [rounds, setRounds] = useState<UnlessRound[]>([]);
345	  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
346	  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
347	  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<{ left: string | null; right: string | null }>({ left: null, right: null });
348	  const [results, setResults] = useState<UnlessResult[]>([]);
349	  const startedAt = useRef(0);
350	  const pickRef = useRef(picked);
351	  pickRef.current = picked;
352	
353	  const commit = useCallback((seconds: number) => {
354	    setRounds(current => {
355	      const round = current[index];
356	      if (round) {
357	        setResults(r => [...r, {
358	          ...round, statement: unlessStatement(round),
359	          chosenLeft: pickRef.current.left, chosenRight: pickRef.current.right, seconds,
360	        }]);
361	      }
362	      return current;
363	    });
364	  }, [index]);
365	
366	  useEffect(() => {
367	    if (phase !== "running") return;
368	    startedAt.current = Date.now();
369	    setElapsed(0);
370	    const tick = window.setInterval(() => {
371	      const ms = Date.now() - startedAt.current;
372	      if (ms >= WINDOW_MS) {
373	        window.clearInterval(tick);
374	        setElapsed(WINDOW_MS);
375	        commit(WINDOW_MS / 1000);
376	        setPhase("stop");
377	      } else setElapsed(ms);
378	    }, 50);
379	    return () => window.clearInterval(tick);
380	  }, [phase, index, commit]);
381	
382	  useEffect(() => {
383	    if (phase !== "stop") return;
384	    const t = window.setTimeout(() => {
385	      setPicked({ left: null, right: null });
386	      if (index + 1 >= rounds.length) setPhase("summary");
387	      else { setIndex(i => i + 1); setPhase("running"); }
388	    }, 900);
389	    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
390	  }, [phase, index, rounds.length]);
391	
392	  const start = () => {
393	    setRounds(buildRound());
394	    setResults([]);
395	    setIndex(0);
396	    setPicked({ left: null, right: null });
397	    setPhase("running");
398	    const saved = readSaved().unless ?? { rounds: 0 };
399	    writeSaved({ unless: { rounds: saved.rounds + 1 } });
400	  };
401	
402	  if (phase === "idle") {
403	    return (
404	      <section className="cir-card">
405	        <p className="cir-prompt">Three unless statements. Five seconds each, no stopping.</p>
406	        <p className="cir-feedback">The arrow is fixed. You only choose which idea goes left and which goes right. Feedback comes after all three.</p>
407	        <div className="cir-actions"><button type="button" className="cir-btn" onClick={start}>Start round</button></div>
408	      </section>
409	    );
410	  }
411	
412	  if (phase === "summary") {
413	    return (
414	      <section className="cir-card">
415	        <p className="cir-prompt">Round review</p>
416	        {results.map((r, i) => {
417	          const answer = unlessAnswer(r);
418	          const contra = contrapositiveOf(answer.left, answer.right);
419	          const ok = (r.chosenLeft === answer.left && r.chosenRight === answer.right)
420	            || (r.chosenLeft === contra.left && r.chosenRight === contra.right);
421	          return (
422	            <div className="cir-summary-row" key={i}>
423	              <span>Statement {i + 1} · {r.seconds.toFixed(1)}s · {ok ? "correct" : "not correct"}</span>
424	              <p style={{ margin: 0 }}>Received: <UnlessText round={r} /></p>
425	              <p style={{ margin: 0 }}>Your diagram: {r.chosenLeft && r.chosenRight
426	                ? <Diagram left={r.chosenLeft} right={r.chosenRight} />
427	                : <em>no answer</em>}</p>
428	              <p style={{ margin: 0 }}>Correct: <Diagram left={answer.left} right={answer.right} /></p>
429	              <p style={{ margin: 0 }}>Contrapositive: <Diagram left={contra.left} right={contra.right} /></p>
430	            </div>
431	          );
432	        })}
433	        <div className="cir-actions"><button type="button" className="cir-btn" onClick={start}>New round</button></div>
434	      </section>
435	    );
436	  }
437	
438	  const round = rounds[index];
439	  const options = [round.x, round.y, `/${round.x}`, `/${round.y}`];
440	  const seconds = Math.min(elapsed, WINDOW_MS) / 1000;
441	
442	  return (
443	    <section className="cir-card">
444	      <p className="cir-meta">Statement {index + 1} of {rounds.length}</p>
445	      <p className="cir-timer">
446	        {phase === "stop" ? "STOP" : seconds.toFixed(1)}
447	        <small>{phase === "stop" ? "next statement" : "GO"}</small>
448	      </p>
449	      <p className="cir-statement"><UnlessText round={round} /></p>
450	      <div className="cir-build">
451	        <div className="cir-slot">{picked.left ? <Tok text={picked.left} /> : <span className="cir-stack-join">left</span>}</div>
452	        <span className="cir-arrow">&rarr;</span>
453	        <div className="cir-slot">{picked.right ? <Tok text={picked.right} /> : <span className="cir-stack-join">right</span>}</div>
454	      </div>
455	      <div className="cir-palette">
456	        {options.map(o => (
457	          <button key={`l-${o}`} type="button" className="cir-token" disabled={phase !== "running"}
458	            onClick={() => setPicked(p => ({ ...p, left: o }))}>Left: <Tok text={o} /></button>
459	        ))}
460	      </div>
461	      <div className="cir-palette">
462	        {options.map(o => (
463	          <button key={`r-${o}`} type="button" className="cir-token" disabled={phase !== "running"}
464	            onClick={() => setPicked(p => ({ ...p, right: o }))}>Right: <Tok text={o} /></button>
465	        ))}
466	      </div>
467	    </section>
468	  );
469	}
470	
471	function UnlessText({ round }: { round: UnlessRound }) {
472	  const text = unlessStatement(round);
473	  const parts = useMemo(() => text.split(/(\/?[A-Z])/).filter(Boolean), [text]);
474	  return (
475	    <span>
476	      {parts.map((p, i) => (/^\/?[A-Z]$/.test(p) ? <Tok key={i} text={p} /> : <span key={i}>{p}</span>))}
477	    </span>
478	  );
479	}

import { safeLazy } from '@/lib/safeLazy';

// Bootcamp pages are full-screen SPA routes; they render blank inside an iframe
// (a fresh context loses the session). Mounting the page component inline in the
// same React tree keeps auth/context, so it renders. Shared by the inline-open
// view and the scaled card preview. safeLazy guards against stale post-deploy
// chunks ("Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'default')").

/** Shared notation: slash-through negation, vertical AND (+) and OR stacks. */

export function Tok({ text }: { text: string }) {
  const negated = text.startsWith("/");
  const label = negated ? text.slice(1) : text;
  return negated ? <span className="cir-neg">{label}</span> : <span>{label}</span>;
}

export function Side({ text }: { text: string }) {
  const join = text.includes(" + ") ? "+" : text.includes(" or ") ? "or" : null;
  if (!join) return <Tok text={text.trim()} />;
  const parts = text.split(join === "+" ? " + " : " or ").map(p => p.trim());
  return (
    <span className="cir-stack">
      {parts.map((part, i) => (
        <span key={`${part}-${i}`}>
          {i > 0 && <span className="cir-stack-join">{join}</span>}
          <span style={{ display: "block" }}><Tok text={part} /></span>
        </span>
      ))}
    </span>
  );
}

export function Diagram({ left, right }: { left: string; right: string }) {
  return (
    <span className="cir-diagram">
      <Side text={left} />
      <span className="cir-arrow">&rarr;</span>
      <Side text={right} />
    </span>
  );
}
/* Conditional Indicators Refresher — self-contained paper-and-ink surface.
   Tokens are scoped to the page root so nothing here leaks into the rest of
   the app, and no component hardcodes a colour utility. */

.cir-root {
  --cir-paper: #f6f2e8;
  --cir-paper-raised: #fdfaf2;
  --cir-ink: #13332a;
  --cir-ink-soft: #3c574c;
  --cir-ink-mute: #6d7f77;
  --cir-rule: #d8cfbb;
  --cir-rule-soft: #e7e0d0;
  --cir-good: #1f5c4a;
  --cir-bad: #8d3a33;
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: var(--cir-paper);
  color: var(--cir-ink);
  font-size: 16px;
}

.cir-shell { max-width: 1040px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 32px clamp(16px, 4vw, 44px) 80px; }

.cir-title {
  font-family: var(--font-display, Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif);
  font-size: clamp(30px, 5vw, 46px);
  line-height: 1.1;
  margin: 0 0 4px;
  font-weight: 600;
}
.cir-sub { color: var(--cir-ink-mute); font-size: 14px; margin: 0 0 24px; }

.cir-tabs { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); padding-bottom: 12px; margin-bottom: 28px; }
.cir-tab {
  border: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); background: transparent; color: var(--cir-ink-soft);
  border-radius: 999px; padding: 8px 16px; font-size: 14px; cursor: pointer;
}
.cir-tab:hover { border-color: var(--cir-ink-mute); }
.cir-tab[aria-selected="true"] { background: var(--cir-ink); color: var(--cir-paper-raised); border-color: var(--cir-ink); }

.cir-lead { font-family: var(--font-display, Georgia, serif); font-size: clamp(20px, 3vw, 26px); margin: 0 0 20px; }

/* Cheat sheet -------------------------------------------------------------- */
.cir-quadrants { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 16px; }
.cir-quad { background: var(--cir-paper-raised); border: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); border-radius: 14px; padding: clamp(18px, 2.4vw, 26px); }
.cir-quad-n { font-size: 11px; letter-spacing: .14em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--cir-ink-mute); }
.cir-quad h2 { font-family: var(--font-display, Georgia, serif); font-size: 22px; margin: 6px 0 14px; font-weight: 600; }
.cir-words { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 16px; }
.cir-word { border: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); border-radius: 8px; padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 14px; background: var(--cir-paper); }
.cir-quad p { color: var(--cir-ink-soft); line-height: 1.65; font-size: 15px; margin: 0; }
.cir-steps { margin: 0; padding-left: 20px; color: var(--cir-ink-soft); line-height: 1.7; font-size: 15px; }
.cir-note { margin-top: 12px; font-size: 14px; color: var(--cir-ink-mute); font-style: italic; }
.cir-legend { margin-top: 22px; border-top: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); padding-top: 18px; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 28px; align-items: center; color: var(--cir-ink-soft); font-size: 14px; }
.cir-legend > div { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }

/* Notation ----------------------------------------------------------------- */
.cir-neg { display: inline-block; position: relative; padding: 0 .08em; white-space: nowrap; }
.cir-neg::after { content: ""; position: absolute; height: 1.5px; width: 150%; left: -25%; top: 50%; background: currentColor; transform: rotate(-52deg); pointer-events: none; }
.cir-stack { display: inline-flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; vertical-align: middle; line-height: 1.25; }
.cir-stack-join { font-size: .85em; color: var(--cir-ink-mute); }
.cir-arrow { margin: 0 .55em; color: var(--cir-ink-soft); }
.cir-diagram { font-size: clamp(18px, 2.4vw, 22px); display: flex; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 4px; }

/* Shared drill furniture --------------------------------------------------- */
.cir-card { background: var(--cir-paper-raised); border: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); border-radius: 14px; padding: clamp(20px, 3vw, 32px); }
.cir-bar { height: 6px; border-radius: 999px; background: var(--cir-rule-soft); overflow: hidden; margin-bottom: 22px; }
.cir-bar > span { display: block; height: 100%; background: var(--cir-good); transition: width .25s ease; }
.cir-prompt { font-family: var(--font-display, Georgia, serif); font-size: clamp(22px, 3.4vw, 30px); line-height: 1.3; margin: 0 0 22px; }
.cir-choices { display: grid; gap: 10px; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr)); }
.cir-choice { text-align: left; border: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); background: var(--cir-paper); color: var(--cir-ink); border-radius: 10px; padding: 14px 16px; font-size: 15px; cursor: pointer; }
.cir-choice:hover:not(:disabled) { border-color: var(--cir-ink-mute); }
.cir-choice:disabled { cursor: default; }
.cir-choice.is-right { border-color: var(--cir-good); color: var(--cir-good); }
.cir-choice.is-wrong { border-color: var(--cir-bad); color: var(--cir-bad); }
.cir-feedback { margin-top: 18px; font-size: 15px; color: var(--cir-ink-soft); }
.cir-feedback strong { color: var(--cir-ink); }
.cir-actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; margin-top: 22px; }
.cir-btn { border: 1px solid var(--cir-ink); background: var(--cir-ink); color: var(--cir-paper-raised); border-radius: 10px; padding: 11px 18px; font-size: 14px; cursor: pointer; }
.cir-btn.ghost { background: transparent; color: var(--cir-ink); border-color: var(--cir-rule); }
.cir-btn:disabled { opacity: .45; cursor: not-allowed; }
.cir-meta { font-size: 13px; color: var(--cir-ink-mute); margin-bottom: 14px; }

/* Flashcards --------------------------------------------------------------- */
.cir-step { border-left: 2px solid var(--cir-rule); padding: 4px 0 4px 16px; margin: 16px 0; color: var(--cir-ink-soft); line-height: 1.7; }
.cir-step b { color: var(--cir-ink); }
.cir-reveal { margin-top: 22px; border-top: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); padding-top: 20px; display: grid; gap: 14px; }
.cir-reveal-row { display: grid; gap: 4px; }
.cir-reveal-row span { font-size: 11px; letter-spacing: .13em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--cir-ink-mute); }
.cir-reveal-row p { margin: 0; line-height: 1.6; }

/* Diagram builder ---------------------------------------------------------- */
.cir-build { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; background: var(--cir-paper); border: 1px dashed var(--cir-rule); border-radius: 12px; padding: 18px; min-height: 84px; }
.cir-slot { min-width: 120px; min-height: 42px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); padding: 6px 10px; font-size: 18px; }
.cir-slot.active { border-color: var(--cir-ink); }
.cir-palette { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; margin-top: 18px; }
.cir-token { border: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); background: var(--cir-paper); color: var(--cir-ink); border-radius: 8px; padding: 8px 14px; font-size: 15px; cursor: pointer; }
.cir-token:hover { border-color: var(--cir-ink-mute); }
.cir-side-toggle { display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 14px; }

/* Unless drill ------------------------------------------------------------- */
.cir-timer { font-family: var(--font-display, Georgia, serif); font-size: clamp(34px, 6vw, 54px); text-align: center; margin: 0; }
.cir-timer small { display: block; font-size: 13px; letter-spacing: .2em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--cir-ink-mute); font-family: inherit; }
.cir-statement { font-family: var(--font-display, Georgia, serif); font-size: clamp(26px, 5vw, 40px); text-align: center; margin: 18px 0 26px; }
.cir-summary-row { border-top: 1px solid var(--cir-rule); padding: 18px 0; display: grid; gap: 8px; }
.cir-summary-row span { font-size: 12px; color: var(--cir-ink-mute); }

@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .cir-quadrants { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); }
  .cir-shell { padding-bottom: 56px; }
}
  /** Optional second accent for bootcamps whose real app uses a two-color identity. */

}

export function bootcampIdFromRoute(route: string): string {
  const routeWithoutQuery = route.split('?')[0]?.split('#')[0] ?? route;
  if (routeWithoutQuery.startsWith('/bootcamp/')) {
    return routeWithoutQuery.replace(/^\/bootcamp\//, '').replace(/\/.*$/, '');
  }
  const topicMatch = BOOTCAMPS.find((b) => {
    const routes = [b.route, ...(b.legacyRoutes ?? [])];
    return routes.some((r) => routeWithoutQuery === r || routeWithoutQuery.startsWith(`${r}/`));
  });
  return topicMatch?.id ?? routeWithoutQuery.replace(/^\/+/, '').replace(/\/.*$/, '');
}

export function findBootcampById(id: string | undefined | null): BootcampDef | undefined {
  if (!id) return undefined;
  return BOOTCAMPS.find((b) => b.id === id);
}

export function findBootcampByRoute(route: string | undefined | null): BootcampDef | undefined {
  if (!route) return undefined;
  const normalized = route.split('?')[0]?.split('#')[0] ?? route;
  return BOOTCAMPS.find((b) => {
    const routes = [b.route, ...(b.legacyRoutes ?? [])];
    return routes.some((r) => normalized === r || normalized.startsWith(`${r}/`));
  });
}

/**
 * Assigning a bootcamp has to actually open it for the student.
 *
 * Three separate places have to agree on one identity: AssignmentBuilder writes
 * the classroom_items row, BootcampGate decides whether to let the student in,
 * and the bootcamp library decides whether to show the card. They agree only if
 * the id derived from the route matches the id stored on the row. These lock
 * that agreement for every registered bootcamp, so a newly added one cannot
 * ship assignable-but-unopenable.
 */

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  BOOTCAMPS,
  bootcampIdFromRoute,
  classroomItemMatchesBootcamp,
  findBootcampById,
  type BootcampDef,
} from './bootcamps';

/** The classroom_items row AssignmentBuilder creates for a bootcamp. */
function assignedRow(bootcamp: BootcampDef) {
  return {
    source_id: bootcamp.route,
    metadata_json: { bootcamp_id: bootcamp.id } as Record<string, unknown>,
  };
}

describe.each(BOOTCAMPS.map((b) => [b.title, b] as const))('%s is assignable', (_title, bootcamp) => {
  it('derives the same id from its route that assignment stores', () => {
    // Topic-family routes resolve through the registry rather than the path.
    expect(bootcampIdFromRoute(bootcamp.route)).toBe(bootcamp.id);
  });

  it('opens the gate for the row assignment writes', () => {
    expect(classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(assignedRow(bootcamp), bootcamp.id)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('opens the gate when matched by route instead of id', () => {
    expect(classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(assignedRow(bootcamp), bootcamp.route)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('opens the gate on metadata alone', () => {
    expect(
      classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(
        { source_id: null, metadata_json: { bootcamp_id: bootcamp.id } },
        bootcamp.id,
      ),
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  it('opens the gate on source_id alone', () => {
    expect(
      classroomItemMatchesBootcamp({ source_id: bootcamp.route, metadata_json: null }, bootcamp.id),
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  it('does not open any other bootcamp', () => {
    for (const other of BOOTCAMPS) {
      if (other.id === bootcamp.id) continue;
      expect(classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(assignedRow(bootcamp), other.id)).toBe(false);
    }
  });
});

describe('bootcamp registry', () => {
  it('has unique ids and routes', () => {
    expect(new Set(BOOTCAMPS.map((b) => b.id)).size).toBe(BOOTCAMPS.length);
    expect(new Set(BOOTCAMPS.map((b) => b.route)).size).toBe(BOOTCAMPS.length);
  });

  it('registers the RC bootcamp so it appears in the assignment picker', () => {
    const rc = findBootcampById('rc-engaging-with-the-passage');
    expect(rc).toBeDefined();
    expect(rc?.route).toBe('/bootcamp/rc-engaging-with-the-passage');
    expect(rc?.title).toBe('Engaging With The Passage');
  });

  it('shows an assigned bootcamp in the student library', () => {
    // The filter src/pages/Bootcamps.tsx applies for a non-admin.
    const rc = findBootcampById('rc-engaging-with-the-passage')!;
    const items = [{ type: 'bootcamp_module', status: 'assigned', ...assignedRow(rc) }];
    const assignedIds = new Set(
      BOOTCAMPS.filter((bc) =>
        items.some(
          (it) =>
            it.type === 'bootcamp_module' &&
            it.status !== 'archived' &&
            classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(it, bc.id),
        ),
      ).map((bc) => bc.id),
    );
    expect([...assignedIds]).toEqual([rc.id]);
  });
});

        items.some(
          (it) =>
            it.type === 'bootcamp_module' &&
            it.status !== 'archived' &&

              {

                metadata_json: it.metadata_json as Record<string, unknown> | null,
              },
              bc.id,
            ),
        ),
      ).map((bc) => bc.id),
    );
  }, [items]);

  const visible = is_admin

/* ------------------------------- Unless drill ------------------------------ */

export type UnlessPattern = 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;

export const UNLESS_LETTERS = ["A", "B", "C", "D", "P", "Q", "R", "S"];

export interface UnlessRound {
  pattern: UnlessPattern;
  x: string;
  y: string;
}

/** Statement text pieces; "/" marks the slashed letter. */
export function unlessStatement({ pattern, x, y }: UnlessRound): string {
  switch (pattern) {
    case 1: return `/${x} unless ${y}`;
    case 2: return `Unless ${y}, ${x}`;
    case 3: return `Unless /${y}, /${x}`;
    case 4: return `${x} unless /${y}`;
  }
}

/**
 * The two complete ideas as they appear in the statement: `after` is the idea
 * that follows "unless" (with any slash it carries), `other` is the rest.
 */
export function unlessParts({ pattern, x, y }: UnlessRound): { after: string; other: string } {
  switch (pattern) {
    case 1: return { after: y, other: `/${x}` };
    case 2: return { after: y, other: x };
    case 3: return { after: `/${y}`, other: `/${x}` };
    case 4: return { after: `/${y}`, other: x };
  }
}

/** The Group 3 move: negate the "unless" idea, put it left, keep the other idea unchanged on the right. */
export function unlessAnswer(round: UnlessRound): { left: string; right: string } {
  const { after, other } = unlessParts(round);
  return { left: negateToken(after), right: other };
}

export type UnlessVerdict = "correct" | "no-answer" | "not-negated" | "reversed" | "both-negated" | "other";

/** Grades a placement. The diagram and its contrapositive both count. */
export function gradeUnless(round: UnlessRound, left: string | null, right: string | null): UnlessVerdict {
  if (!left || !right) return "no-answer";
  const { after, other } = unlessParts(round);
  const is = (l: string, r: string) => left === l || right === r ? left === l && right === r : false;
  const either = (l: string, r: string) => is(l, r) || is(negateToken(r), negateToken(l));
  if (either(negateToken(after), other)) return "correct";
  if (either(after, other)) return "not-negated";
  if (either(other, negateToken(after))) return "reversed";
  if (either(negateToken(after), negateToken(other))) return "both-negated";
  return "other";
}

export function negateToken(token: string): string {
  return token.startsWith("/") ? token.slice(1) : `/${token}`;
}

export function contrapositiveOf(left: string, right: string): { left: string; right: string } {
  return { left: negateToken(right), right: negateToken(left) };
}

Used as a tab of the Conditional Indicators\n   Refresher and as its own assignable page (/unless-drill). The statements are\n   the refresher's originals: three of the four unless patterns per round, with\n   fresh letters each time. */\n\nconst KEY = \"lsatu.unless-drill.v1\";\nconst PACES = [\n  { ms: 5000, label: \"5 seconds\" },\n  { ms: 10000, label: \"10 seconds (warm-up)\" },\n] as const;\nconst READY_STEPS = 3;\nconst READY_STEP_MS = 550;\nconst BETWEEN_MS = 850;\nconst WARN_MS = 1500;\n\ninterface Saved { pace?: number; rounds?: number; bestPerfectAvg?: number }\n\nfunction readSaved(): Saved {\n  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || \"{}\") as Saved; } catch { return {}; }\n}\nfunction writeSaved(patch: Saved) {\n  try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify({ ...readSaved(), ...patch })); } catch { /* storage unavailable */ }\n}\n\nfunction shuffle<T>(items: T[]): T[] {\n  const a = [...items];\n  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];\n  }\n  return a;\n}\n\nfunction buildRound(): UnlessRound[] {\n  const patterns = shuffle<UnlessPattern>([1, 2, 3, 4]).slice(0, 3);\n  const letters = shuffle(UNLESS_LETTERS);\n  return shuffle(patterns).map((pattern, i) => ({\n    pattern,\n    x: letters[(i * 2) % letters.length],\n    y: letters[(i * 2 + 1) % letters.length],\n  }));\n}\n\ninterface UnlessResult extends UnlessRound {\n  chosenLeft: string | null;\n  chosenRight: string | null;\n  seconds: number;\n  timedOut: boolean;\n  verdict: UnlessVerdict;\n}\n\ntype Phase = \"idle\" | \"ready\" | \"running\" | \"between\" | \"summary\";\ntype Placed = { left: string | null; right: string | null };\nconst EMPTY: Placed = { left: null, right: null };\n\nconst VERDICT_TEXT: Record<Exclude<UnlessVerdict, \"correct\">, string> = {\n  \"no-answer\": \"Time ran out before both sides were filled.\",\n  \"not-negated\": \"You kept the unless idea as written. [round.x, round.y, `/${round.x}`, `/${round.y}`] : []),\n    [round],\n  );\n\n  // Keys: 1-4 place a token, Backspace takes the last one back, Enter locks in.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (phase !== \"running\") return;\n    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      const n = Number(e.key);\n      if (n >= 1 && n <= options.length) { place(options[n - 1]); e.preventDefault(); }\n      else if (e.key === \"Backspace\") { clearLast(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      else if (e.key === \"Enter\" && placedRef.current.left && placedRef.current.right) { settle(false); e.preventDefault(); }\n    };\n    window.addEventListener(\"keydown\", onKey);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\"keydown\", onKey);\n  }, [phase, options, place, clearLast, settle]);\n\n  /* --------------------------------- Idle --------------------------------- */\n  if (phase === \"idle\") {\n    return (\n      <section className=\"cir-card\">\n        <p className=\"cir-prompt\">Three unless statements. Beat the clock on each one.</p>\n        <div className=\"ud-move\">\n          <p className=\"ud-move-title\">The Group 3 move</p>\n          <ol className=\"cir-steps\">\n            <li>Find the idea right after <strong>unless</strong>.</li>\n            <li>Negate it.</li>\n            <li>Put it on the left of the arrow.</li>\n            <li>Keep the other idea exactly as written on the right.</li>\n          </ol>\n          <div className=\"ud-example\">\n            <span className=\"ud-example-label\">Example</span>\n            <span className=\"ud-example-statement\"><UnlessText round={{ pattern: 1, x: \"A\", y: \"B\" }} /></span>\n            <span className=\"ud-example-row\">After unless: <Tok text=\"B\" />. Negate it: <Tok text=\"/B\" />. <Tok text=\"/A\" /> stays as written.</span>\n            <span className=\"ud-example-row\"><Diagram left=\"/B\" right=\"/A\" /></span>\n            <span className=\"ud-example-row ud-muted\">The contrapositive, <Diagram left=\"A\" right=\"B\" />, also counts.</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <p className=\"cir-feedback\">\n          Tap the idea that goes left, then the idea that goes right. Lock it in as soon as you are sure.\n          Your review comes after all three.\n        </p>\n        <div className=\"ud-pace\" role=\"radiogroup\" aria-label=\"Time per statement\">\n          {PACES.map(p => (\n            <button key={p.ms} type=\"button\" role=\"radio\" aria-checked={pace === p.ms}\n              className=\"cir-tab\" onClick={() => choosePace(p.ms)}>{p.label}</button>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"cir-actions\"><button type=\"button\" className=\"cir-btn\" onClick={() => begin(buildRound())}>Start round</button></div>\n        {best !== undefined && <p className=\"cir-meta ud-best\">Fastest perfect round: {best.toFixed(1)}s per statement</p>}\n      </section>\n    );\n  }\n\n  /* --------------------------------- Ready -------------------------------- */\n  if (phase === \"ready\") {\n    return (\n      <section className=\"cir-card ud-stage\">\n        <p className=\"cir-meta\">Get ready</p>\n        <p className=\"ud-ready\" aria-live=\"assertive\">{ready > 0 ? ready : \"Go\"}</p>\n      </section>\n    );\n  }\n\n  /* -------------------------------- Review -------------------------------- */\n  if (phase === \"summary\") {\n    const correct = results.filter(r => r.verdict === \"correct\").length;\n    const avg = results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.seconds, 0) / Math.max(results.length, 1);\n    const missed: UnlessRound[] = results\n      .filter(r => r.verdict !== \"correct\")\n      .map(({ pattern, x, y }) => ({ pattern, x, y }));\n    return (\n      <section className=\"cir-card\">\n        <p className=\"cir-prompt\">Round review</p>\n        <div className=\"ud-score\">\n          <div><b>{correct} of {results.length}</b><span>correct</span></div>\n          <div><b>{avg.toFixed(1)}s</b><span>average per statement</span></div>\n          {best !== undefined && <div><b>{best.toFixed(1)}s</b><span>fastest perfect round</span></div>}\n        </div>\n        {results.map((r, i) => <ReviewRow key={i} n={i + 1} result={r} />)}\n        <div className=\"cir-actions\">\n          {missed.length > 0 && (\n            <button type=\"button\" className=\"cir-btn\" onClick={() => begin(shuffle(missed))}>\n              Retry the {missed.length === 1 ? \"one I missed\" : `${missed.length} I missed`}\n            </button>\n          )}\n          <button type=\"button\" className={`cir-btn${missed.length ? \" ghost\" : \"\"}`} onClick={() => begin(buildRound())}>New round</button>\n          <button type=\"button\" className=\"cir-btn ghost\" onClick={() => setPhase(\"idle\")}>Change pace</button>\n        </div>\n      </section>\n    );\n  }\n\n  /* --------------------------- Running / between -------------------------- */\n  const last = results[results.length - 1];\n  const live = phase === \"running\";\n  const fraction = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, remaining / pace));\n  const warn = live && remaining <= WARN_MS;\n  const canLock = live && !!placed.left && !!placed.right;\n\n  return (\n    <section className=\"cir-card ud-stage\">\n      <div className=\"ud-head\">\n        <span className=\"cir-meta\">Statement {index + 1} of {rounds.length}</span>\n        <span className={`ud-count${warn ? \" is-warn\" : \"\"}`} aria-hidden=\"true\">\n          {live ? (remaining / 1000).toFixed(1) : last?.timedOut ? \"Time\" : \"Locked\"}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n      <div className={`ud-clock${warn ? \" is-warn\" : \"\"}`} role=\"timer\" aria-label=\"Time left\">\n        <span style={{ transform: `scaleX(${live ? fraction : last?.timedOut ? 0 : fraction})` }} />\n      </div>\n\n      <p className=\"cir-statement\">{round && <UnlessText round={round} />}</p>\n\n      <div className=\"cir-build ud-build\">\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"cir-slot ud-slot\" disabled={!live || !placed.left}\n          aria-label={placed.left ? `Left side ${placed.left}, tap to clear` : \"Left side, empty\"}\n          onClick={() => setPlaced(p => ({ ...p, left: null }))}>\n          {placed.left ? <Tok text={placed.left} /> : <span className=\"cir-stack-join\">left</span>}\n        </button>\n        <span className=\"cir-arrow\">&rarr;</span>\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"cir-slot ud-slot\" disabled={!live || !placed.right}\n          aria-label={placed.right ? `Right side ${placed.right}, tap to clear` : \"Right side, empty\"}\n          onClick={() => setPlaced(p => ({ ...p, right: null }))}>\n          {placed.right ? <Tok text={placed.right} /> : <span className=\"cir-stack-join\">right</span>}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"ud-tokens\">\n        {options.map((o, i) => (\n          <button key={o} type=\"button\" className=\"cir-token ud-token\" disabled={!live || (!!placed.left && !!placed.right)}\n            onClick={() => place(o)}>\n            <Tok text={o} /><kbd>{i + 1}</kbd>\n          </button>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"cir-actions ud-lock\">\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"cir-btn\" disabled={!canLock} onClick={() => settle(false)}>Lock in</button>\n      </div>\n      <p className=\"cir-meta ud-hint\">Tap a side to clear it. Keys: 1 to 4 place, Backspace undoes, Enter locks in.</p>\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ReviewRow({ n, result }: { n: number; result: UnlessResult }) {\n  const answer = unlessAnswer(result);\n  const contra = contrapositiveOf(answer.left, answer.right);\n  const { after, other } = unlessParts(result);\n  const ok = result.verdict === \"correct\";\n  return (\n    <div className={`cir-summary-row ud-row${ok ? \" is-right\" : \" is-wrong\"}`}>\n      <div className=\"cir-summary-meta\">\n        Statement {n} · {result.timedOut ? \"time ran out\" : `${result.seconds.toFixed(1)}s`} · {ok ? \"correct\" : \"not correct\"}\n      </div>\n      <p className=\"ud-row-statement\"><UnlessText round={result} /></p>\n      <div className=\"ud-row-grid\">\n        <span className=\"ud-row-label\">Your diagram</span>\n        <span>{result.chosenLeft && result.chosenRight\n          ? <Diagram left={result.chosenLeft} right={result.chosenRight} />\n          : <em>no answer</em>}</span>\n        <span className=\"ud-row-label\">Correct</span>\n        <span><Diagram left={answer.left} right={answer.right} /></span>\n        <span className=\"ud-row-label\">Also counts</span>\n        <span><Diagram left={contra.left} right={contra.right} /> <span className=\"ud-muted\">(contrapositive)</span></span>\n      </div>\n      {!ok && <p className=\"ud-why\">{VERDICT_TEXT[result.verdict as Exclude<UnlessVerdict, \"correct\">]}</p>}\n      <p className=\"ud-steps\">\n        After unless: <Tok text={after} />. Negate it: <Tok text={negateToken(after)} />\n        {after.startsWith(\"/\") ? \" (the two slashes cancel)\" : \"\"}. That goes left.{\" \"}\n        <Tok text={other} /> stays as written on the right.\n      </p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction UnlessText({ round }: { round: UnlessRound }) {\n  const text = unlessStatement(round);\n  const parts = useMemo(() => text.split(/(\\/?[A-Z])/).filter(Boolean), [text]);\n  return (\n    <span>\n      {parts.map((p, i) => (/^\\/?[A-Z]$/.test(p) ? <Tok key={i} text={p} /> : <span key={i}>{p}</span>))}\n    </span>\n  );\n}\n"}

'/' + n.textContent : n.textContent);\n  const text = toks.join('');\n  // tokens with letters\n  const letters = toks.filter(t => /^\\/?[A-Z]$/.test(t) && t !== 'U');\n  let after, other;\n  if (text.startsWith('Unless')) { after = letters[0]; other = letters[1]; } else { other = letters[0]; after = letters[1]; }\n  const neg = t => t.startsWith('/') ? t.slice(1) : '/' + t;\n  const want = [neg(after), other];\n  const opts = [...document.querySelectorAll('.ud-token')].map(b => (b.querySelector('.cir-neg') ? '/' : '') + b.firstChild.textContent);\n  for (const w of want) window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: String(opts.indexOf(w) + 1) }));\n  await sleep(50);\n  window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Enter' }));\n  log.push(text + ' => ' + want.join(' -> '));\n  await sleep(1000);\n}\nawait sleep(500);\n[log, document.querySelector('.ud-score')?.innerText, localStorage.getItem('lsatu.unless-drill.v1')]"}

## assistant

The Unless drill now has its own page, and you can assign it on its own. It isn't live yet: it's in [PR #205](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/205), which can be merged cleanly and is waiting on you.

**Same questions.** Each round is still three of the four "unless" patterns, with new letters each time and five seconds per statement. I didn't add "until" or "without." The Unless tab inside the refresher now uses the same rebuilt drill.

**Where it lives:** `/unless-drill`. It shows up as **Unless Drill** in Desk → Library → Bootcamps, where you assign it like the refresher. The default end date is 7 days after the start. Students only see it once it's assigned to them.

**The timer and drill, rebuilt:**
- **Clock:** a 3-2-1 "get ready" count, then a countdown with a bar that drains and turns red in the last 1.5 seconds. "Lock in" ends a statement early, so the time recorded is how fast they actually were. There's also an optional 10-second warm-up pace.
- **Answering:** students tap an idea to fill the left side, then the right. Tapping a side clears it. Keys 1–4, Backspace and Enter work too.
- **Before starting:** the four steps of the Group 3 move, with a worked example.
- **Review:** score, average time and fastest perfect round. Each statement shows the correct diagram and the contrapositive (both count as correct). It also names the mistake: didn't negate, wrong direction, negated both, or ran out of time. Then it works the move through on that statement. "Retry the ones I missed" reruns only the missed statements.

**What was broken before:**
- A style rule shrank the statement and diagrams in the review to tiny grey text. That's the garbled "Received" and "Correct" in your screenshot.
- The drill could record each answer twice.
- The step lists had lost their numbers.

**Checks:** the code compiles with no type or lint errors, and 66 tests pass, including 9 new ones for the answer key and grading. I ran the drill in a browser and checked a timeout, a wrong-direction answer, an all-correct round and phone width.

**Not checked:** I couldn't open the real signed-in `/unless-drill` page. I tested the drill by mounting the page on its own in a local test file, which I've deleted.

To get it to students I need two things from you:
1. **OK to merge PR #205 and publish.** Publishing the site doesn't spend Lovable credits.
2. **Which students to assign it to.**

Base directory for this skill: C:\Users\Joshu\.claude\skills\grill-me

# Grill Me

One skill, self-contained. It holds both the **interview method** (Step 2) and the **capture discipline** (Steps 1 and 3): every answer lands in a durable markdown file, so nothing is lost as context fills up. It does not call the vendor `grilling` skill: the method is written inline below so this skill can add the capture steps around it and keep working even if `grilling` changes upstream.

If the topic is a feature, component, or other implementation work rather than an open-ended plan or decision, `brainstorming` runs a similar interview but gates on a written, approved design doc before any code — reach for that instead when the destination is implementation.

## Step 1: set up the capture file (BEFORE the first question)

1. Read the applicable `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md`, then only the context pages and prior captures relevant to the topic. Starter-kit context lives in `context/`; in an established project follow its declared routes instead of creating a duplicate knowledge store. Reuse facts already supplied; ask about gaps, changes, decisions, and tradeoffs. Installing the skill does not start an interview.
2. Create the capture file at `brainstorms/{YYYY-MM-DD}-{topic-slug}.md` (create `brainstorms/` if missing). Every capture lives here, one predictable home regardless of topic. Do NOT scatter captures into project folders. A polished deliverable (plan, map, spec) may later move into the relevant `projects/` folder; the raw capture always stays in `brainstorms/`.
   - Get today's real date from the clock or shell (`Get-Date -Format yyyy-MM-dd` in PowerShell, `date +%F` in Bash).
   - Never overwrite an existing capture. Same date and topic: add a time or unique suffix and create exclusively. Resume an existing capture only when the user asks or clearly refers to that session; read it first and continue its question numbering.
   - No topic supplied or inferable: create an `untitled-discovery` capture with goal pending, ask what to explore, record the chosen topic and goal in that same file.
3. Write the header immediately: title, date, goal of the session, empty "Open flags" section.
4. Tell the user where you are saving, in one line.

## Step 2: run the interview

Interview the user relentlessly until you reach a shared understanding. Map this as a **design tree**: every decision branches into the decisions that hang off it.

Work the tree in **rounds**. The **frontier** is every decision whose prerequisites are already settled: the questions you can ask _now_ without guessing at answers you have not heard yet. Ask the whole frontier in one round: number each question and give your recommended answer. Then wait for the user's answers before the next round.

Format a round like so:

```
**Q1 - <question title>**: <question body, may be multiple paragraphs, including choices>

Recommended: <your recommended answer>

---

**Q2 - <question title>**: ...

Recommended: ...
```

Each round the user answers reshapes the tree: settled decisions push the frontier outward and unblock questions that depended on them. Recompute the frontier and ask the next round. A question whose answer depends on another question still open in this round belongs to a _later_ round, not this one.

Finding _facts_ is your job, never the user's. When a frontier question needs a fact from the environment (filesystem, tools, live site), look it up or dispatch a sub-agent; never ask the user for anything you could find yourself. Do not block on it: a running exploration is an unsettled prerequisite, so only the questions downstream of it wait; ask the rest of the frontier now. The _decisions_ are the user's: put each to them and wait.

The session is done when the frontier is empty: every branch visited, nothing silently assumed. Do not act on it until the user confirms you have reached a shared understanding.

Two adjustments the capture layer imposes on the method:

- For **decisions**, give a recommended answer grounded in context and label it as a recommendation. For **personal or business facts**, ask neutrally. Never lead with invented facts, and never save your inference as the user's answer.
- When the user **cannot answer** something, capture it as a flag with the right owner and move on. Do not stall the round on it.

## Step 3: the checkpoint rule (non-negotiable)

After EVERY round of answers, BEFORE you ask the next round:

- Append one structured entry per answered question: the question, the key facts and decisions from the answer (in the user's words where wording matters), and any flags (things they could not answer, plus who should).
- Update the running summary when a later answer changes it. Preserve the original entry, mark it superseded, and link to the correcting answer so the reasoning stays traceable.
- Read back the saved entries before continuing. If the write fails, say so, keep the answers visible in chat, and do not claim they were saved or collect more answers without a working checkpoint.
- Only then ask the next round.

Never let two rounds pile up in your head before writing. The file, not your context, is the source of truth. Never make the user ask you to save progress.

Keep user-confirmed facts, tentative ideas, your recommendations, and unresolved questions distinguishable in the capture.

## Capture file structure

```
# {Topic}: Brainstorm / Discovery Notes
Date: {date} · Goal: {one line}
Status: in progress / paused / complete
Context sources: {relevant existing pages, if any}

## Summary / key decisions
(running synthesis, updated as you go)

## Q&A log
### Round 1
#### Q1 - {topic}
- Asked: {question}
- Recommended: {your recommendation, labeled}
- Captured: {user-confirmed facts and decisions, in their words where it matters}
- Tentative: {unconfirmed ideas, clearly labeled}
- Flags: {open item -> owner}
#### Q2 - ...
### Round 2
...

## Open flags (pending input)
- {item} -> {who can answer}
```

## At the end or on pause

Respect an explicit stop immediately; do not add a new question. Near the end, offer a completeness backstop ("anything we haven't touched?"). Then:

- Read the capture for contradictions or gaps. Mark unresolved conflicts explicitly; do not choose a business fact on the user's behalf. Update status and resume point.
- For sessions explicitly about building or updating AI OS context, merge user-confirmed durable facts and preferences into the appropriate existing context page, with a dated link back to the capture. Preserve unrelated content. Record confirmed meaningful decisions in the existing decision log without duplicating entries. Unknowns, brainstorm ideas, and your recommendations stay labeled in the capture until confirmed.
- For a plan or design interview without a request to update canonical context, the raw capture is the output. Suggest `/link` for routing a useful result, or `/level-up` for turning a selected opportunity into one improvement. Do not run either automatically.
- Ensure the operating manual or an existing index routes to `brainstorms/` when capture routing is absent, using the project's conventions and preserving shared manual parity. Individual captures do not each need a root-manual entry. Raw captures are dated interview evidence, not automatically current business truth.
- Give a short recap: clickable capture path, context pages actually updated, remaining flags, next step or resume point.

Do not act on the plan until the user confirms you have reached a shared understanding.

## Boundaries and verification

- Scope is the local interview capture and any requested context updates. No global-memory edits, publication, messages, or external-system changes. Do not request or save credentials; use references or redactions for secrets.
- The starter kit ignores `brainstorms/` in Git by default. This does not remove captures already tracked in an existing repository.
- Validate a fresh session, a same-day name collision, a resume, a correcting answer, a stop request, and a failed write. Verify that only confirmed facts reach canonical context and that earlier answers remain traceable.

I'm saving this session to [brainstorms/2026-10-12-unless-drill.md](brainstorms/2026-10-12-unless-drill.md).

Before the questions, I need to own a mistake. Your answer key showed me something I got backwards in PR #205:

- **"Correct" shows the other form, not yours.** For "~A, unless B." your key says **A → B**. My review labels /B → /A as "Correct" and puts A → B under "Also counts." Both are graded right, but the one it calls correct isn't the one in your key.
- **The steps don't produce your key.** I wrote "negate the idea after unless, put it on the left." Your key comes from the opposite move: negate the *other* idea and put it on the left, and keep the unless idea exactly as written on the right.

Nothing has shipped, so I'll fix both in the same PR. Here's what I need from you:

**Q1 - Four statements per round**: Every round shows all four statements, a, b, c and d, instead of three of the four. The order changes each round so students can't memorize the sequence. Your instruction 2 ("switch up which three") no longer applies.

Recommended: all four every round, in a new order each time.

---

**Q2 - Which form counts as "Correct"**: Your key's form (A → B for "~A, unless B.") is shown as the answer. The contrapositive (/B → /A) is still marked right, but labeled "also correct (contrapositive)." Or do you want only your key's form to count?

Recommended: your key's form is the answer, and the contrapositive is also marked right.

---

**Q3 - How the steps are worded**: The start screen and the review would teach the move that gives your key: "1. Find the idea that is *not* attached to unless. 2. Negate it. 3. Put it on the left. 4. Put the unless idea on the right, exactly as written." Is that how you say it, or do you have your own wording?

The revised local copy asks what the learner needs to decide: “Who does the rule say will receive a wristband?”, “What kind of key is needed to open the cabinet?”, and “What kind of pass does entry require?” The lesson demonstrates why before noon, current, registered and with yellow tags matter. Dropping one of those words changes which cases the rule covers. An arrow is checked by imagining its left condition and asking what the sentence then establishes, rather than treating the first English clause as the left side.

Joshua also asked for the mixed drill to train a skill and build a habit, with one question visible and a progress bar. The current local response uses five stages with a concrete purpose for each: retain the full conditions, build a diagram, attempt a translation before supplied labels, compare meanings, then transfer to unfamiliar cases. Error feedback distinguishes dropped qualifications, reversed roles, required versus enough and changed meaning. A fresh question checks the repaired distinction; correcting the exposed answer alone does not demonstrate transfer. Written translations are compared by the learner and are not represented as automatically graded.

Keep modality in the examples. An electric vehicle that is denied permission disproves the claim that being electric guarantees permission; a vehicle merely staying outside does not. A required action is an obligation, not a fact proving compliance. Confirmation practice for that mistake must test another obligation rather than an unrelated wording comparison.

These are demonstrated corrections and the current implementation response, not approval of every exact replacement sentence or proof that the voice matches Joshua identically. The [selected voice standard](../references/lsat-teaching-voice-standard.md) still governs wording, cadence, concrete reasoning and scope. [Current session record](log.md) distinguishes local work from publication.

Further steering in the same session: “Keep the complete condition” was itself awkward as a visible heading. Ask who or what the particular rule covers instead. Joshua also rejected the landing page's total question/stage counts, per-stage counts, long stage list and padded explanation of repairs. A shorter launch should explain the immediate task; each stage should introduce its purpose and instructions when the learner reaches it. That latest flow revision was requested while final verification was still pending, so this record does not certify its implementation.


### RC actions should describe what the student gets (2026-10-07)

Joshua rejected Inspect set as unclear and awkward. Use Preview drill for viewing the exact selected passages and questions before starting. Keep Start this drill distinct and explain whether answers are hidden and whether work is saved. Generic Menu is also insufficient where the actual action is Question details. This correction does not justify adding more launch instructions, repeated summaries, or unrelated assigned work inside RC setup.

## assistant

I took out the written example and put a short looping clip in its place. It plays one statement on the real drill screen, so students see exactly what they're about to do. It's pushed to [PR #205](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/205).

**What the clip shows** (about 7 seconds, on repeat, starting as soon as the page opens):
1. "/K, unless M." appears and the clock starts draining.
2. K gets tapped and drops into the left side.
3. M gets tapped and drops into the right side.
4. Lock in gets pressed.
5. "Correct: K → M" appears.

A one-line caption under the clip says what's happening at each step. It uses its own letters (K and M), not A and B.

**The start screen now has:** the title, the four steps, the clip, the tap/lock-in line, the pace choice, your "start every study session" line, and Start round.

**Checked:** type check and lint pass. I watched it in the browser and every step played in order, ending on "Correct: K → M." For students whose device is set to reduce motion, it shows the finished step instead of animating. I didn't check that setting in the browser.

It's still not live for students. Say merge and publish, and tell me which students should get it.

I added all three things and pushed them to [PR #205](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/205).

**Play button:** the clip no longer starts by itself. It sits faded behind a round play button that says "Watch a preview." Pressing it plays the walkthrough once, then the button comes back as "Replay preview."

**Preview label:** a small "PREVIEW" tag sits on the corner of the clip, so it doesn't look like the drill has already started.

**10 seconds first, then 5:**
- A new student starts at 10 seconds. The 5-second option shows "5 seconds (locked)" and can't be picked. Under it: "Get all four right at 10 seconds to unlock 5 seconds."
- A perfect round of all four at 10 seconds unlocks it. The review then says "All four right at 10 seconds. 5 seconds is unlocked." and offers a "Try 5 seconds" button.
- "Retry the ones I missed" doesn't count toward the unlock, because it's only part of a round.
- The "You hit the goal" message now only appears for a perfect round of all four at 5 seconds.
- I updated the library card to say "10 seconds, then 5."

**Checked in the browser, starting fresh:**
- 5 seconds was locked.
- The play button played every step and then switched to Replay.
- A perfect 10-second round unlocked 5 seconds and showed the message.

Type check, lint and all tests pass.

**One limit you should know:** the unlock is saved in the student's browser, not on their account. If a student switches devices or clears their browser data, they'd have to pass at 10 seconds again. Saving it to the account would need a small database change. Tell me if you want that.

-- Topic-bootcamp progress tracking. Two thin tables:
--
-- 1. chapter_progress
--    One row per (user, topic_tag, section_id) the first time a student
--    scrolls a chapter section into view. Updated on subsequent views.
--    Powers the Teacher Workspace Bootcamps dashboard "sections viewed".
--
-- 2. flashcard_reviews
--    One row per flashcard review event. SRS-compatible: stores the rating
--    multiplier the student picked, the next-due timestamp, and the
--    cumulative review_count. Aggregations (mastery %, due count, avg
--    interval) compose at read time so future SRS changes do not break
--    historical data.
--
-- Both tables: RLS so a student can read+write only their own rows; admin
-- can read everyone via public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin').

-- ── chapter_progress ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.chapter_progress (
  user_id          uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  topic_tag        text        NOT NULL,
  section_id       text        NOT NULL,
  first_viewed_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_viewed_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  view_count       integer     NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, topic_tag, section_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS chapter_progress_topic_user_idx
  ON public.chapter_progress (topic_tag, user_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS chapter_progress_topic_last_viewed_idx
  ON public.chapter_progress (topic_tag, last_viewed_at DESC);

ALTER TABLE public.chapter_progress ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "chapter_progress_select_own" ON public.chapter_progress;
CREATE POLICY "chapter_progress_select_own"
  ON public.chapter_progress FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "chapter_progress_select_admin" ON public.chapter_progress;
CREATE POLICY "chapter_progress_select_admin"
  ON public.chapter_progress FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "chapter_progress_insert_own" ON public.chapter_progress;
CREATE POLICY "chapter_progress_insert_own"
  ON public.chapter_progress FOR INSERT
  TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "chapter_progress_update_own" ON public.chapter_progress;
CREATE POLICY "chapter_progress_update_own"
  ON public.chapter_progress FOR UPDATE
  TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id)
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

The drill's goal.\n   Signed out (or offline), the browser copy is used so the page still works. */\n\nconst TOPIC = \"unless-drill\";\nexport type UnlessMilestone = \"cleared-10s\" | \"cleared-5s\";\nconst LOCAL_KEY = \"lsatu.unless-drill.milestones.v1\";\n\nfunction readLocal(): UnlessMilestone[] {\n  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_KEY) || \"[]\") as UnlessMilestone[]; } catch { return []; }\n}\nfunction writeLocal(list: UnlessMilestone[]) {\n  try { localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_KEY, JSON.stringify(list)); } catch { /* storage unavailable */ }\n}\n\nexport function useUnlessMilestones() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const [milestones, setMilestones] = useState<Set<UnlessMilestone>>(() => new Set(readLocal()));\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!!user);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!user) { setLoading(false); return; }\n    let cancelled = false;\n    setLoading(true);\n    supabase\n      .from(\"chapter_progress\")\n      .select(\"section_id\")\n      .eq(\"user_id\", user.id)\n      .eq(\"topic_tag\", TOPIC)\n      .then(({ data, error }) => {\n        if (cancelled) return;\n        // The account is the authority; only fall back to the browser copy on error.\n        if (!error) setMilestones(new Set((data ?? []).map(r => r.section_id as UnlessMilestone)));\n        setLoading(false);\n      });\n    return () => { cancelled = true; };\n  }, [user]);\n\n  const record = useCallback(async (m: UnlessMilestone) => {\n    setMilestones(prev => new Set(prev).add(m));\n    writeLocal([...new Set([...readLocal(), m])]);\n    if (!user) return;\n    const now = new Date().toISOString();\n    const { error } = await supabase.from(\"chapter_progress\").insert({\n      user_id: user.id, topic_tag: TOPIC, section_id: m,\n      first_viewed_at: now, last_viewed_at: now, view_count: 1,\n    });\n    // 23505: already recorded, which is fine.\n    if (error && error.code !== \"23505\") console.warn(\"Unless drill: could not save milestone\", error.message);\n  }, [user]);\n\n  return { milestones, loading, record };\n}\n"}
